## Foundation-First Sprint Lock

Status: **FOUNDATION-FIRST LOCK ACTIVE**. All non-foundation work is POST-FOUNDATION BACKLOG and blocked until FOUNDATION GO. Read `docs/architecture/foundation-first-sprint-lock-governance.md` before planning or implementing sprint work.

## RME Module (Sprint 20)

**Modules:** `ClinicVisit`, `MedicalRecord`, `Odontogram`, `RmeInvoice` under `app/Modules/`.

**Routes:** `rme.*` prefix in `routes/web.php`.

**Workflow:** Admin creates visit → Doctor odontogram + handwriting RME → finalize →
`cashier_pending` → Cashier invoice + full payment → `completed` → print receipt.

**Visit statuses:** `registered`, `waiting`, `in_progress`, `cashier_pending`, `completed`, `cancelled`.

**Invoice statuses:** `DRAFT`, `UNPAID`, `PAID`, `VOID`.

**Rules:**
- Handwriting RM is the **primary doctor-facing clinical input**; SOAP fields are optional legacy structured fields hidden from doctor UI.
- Handwriting PNG **mandatory** before RME finalization; immutable after finalize.
- Initial service is triage-only — **no billing**.
- Cashier billing requires finalized RME + `cashier_pending` visit (`manage_rme_billing`).
- **Full payment only** in pilot — partial/cicilan deferred.
- Print via browser `window.print()` — no server PDF.

**Permissions:** `view_clinic_visits`, `manage_clinic_visits`, `manage_rme_billing`.

**Pilot doc:** `docs/sprint_20_rme_limited_pilot_summary.md`

**Pilot master data import:** use `php artisan rme:import-pilot-backup` (see `docs/rme_pilot_backup_import_guide.md`). Never restore backup SQL directly over the Sprint 20 database.

**UI:** All RME views use TailAdmin components (`x-ui.card`, `x-ui.table`, `x-ui.badge`, `x-ui.button`, `x-settings-shell`). UI modernization merged into `feature/sprint-20-rme-core` (merge commit `8246008`, tag `sprint-20-rme-ui-modernization-merged`).

**Sprint 20 closure (2026-06-11):** Final branch `feature/sprint-20-rme-core`. Closure tag `sprint-20-rme-core-ui-complete`. Full suite: 1842 passed / 6290 assertions. RME suite: 283 passed / 718 assertions. Do NOT re-open SOAP in doctor UI — handwriting RM is the primary clinical input and SOAP is hidden by design. Full-payment-only rule remains in force (partial/cicilan deferred to Sprint 21). Closure report: `docs/sprint_20_final_closure_report.md`.

**Sprint 21 planning (2026-06-11):** Planning branch `feature/sprint-21-planning`. Theme: RME Advanced Workflow + Pilot Deployment. Planning doc: `docs/sprint_21_planning.md`. First implementation phase: Phase 21.1 RME → Lab integration architecture (design only), then Phase 21.2 lab order generation (tests-first). SOAP doctor UI remains hidden. Full-payment-only behavior is the baseline — partial/cicilan changes begin only when Phase 21.4 is explicitly approved. No feature code added in planning phase.

**Sprint 21 Phase 21.1 — RME → Lab architecture (2026-06-11):** Branch `feature/sprint-21-rme-lab-architecture`. Tag `sprint-21-phase-21-1-rme-lab-architecture`. Architecture doc: `docs/sprint_21_rme_lab_integration_architecture.md`. Design only — no code changed. Key decisions: (1) RME → Lab trigger is after `RmePaymentService::pay()` sets invoice to `PAID`. (2) Pilot strategy: create `LabCaseCandidate` staging record first (not direct LabOrder) — `LabOrderItem` uses `lab_service_id` but RME items use `treatment_id`; no mapping exists yet. (3) One candidate per eligible `trx_rme_invoice_items` row where `mst_treatments.requires_lab = true` (column already exists). (4) Idempotent via `UNIQUE(rme_invoice_item_id)` on `trx_lab_case_candidates`. (5) Payment commits first; candidate generation is post-commit (failure does not roll back payment). (6) `branch_id` copied from RME invoice; validated against `BranchContext::requireId()`. (7) Do NOT create `trx_payments` (lab billing) records from RME payment — `trx_rme_payments` only. Phase 21.2 unblocked after project owner approves this architecture.

**Sprint 21 Phase 21.2 — Lab Case Candidate generation (2026-06-11):** Branch `feature/sprint-21-lab-case-candidates`. Tag `sprint-21-phase-21-2-lab-case-candidates`. Implements the first functional RME → Lab integration layer. New table `trx_lab_case_candidates` (migration `2026_06_14_210001`). New model `App\Modules\LabOrder\Models\LabCaseCandidate` (status constants: `pending_review`, `converted_to_lab_order`, `rejected`, `cancelled`). New service `App\Modules\RmeInvoice\Services\RmeLabIntegrationService` — `generateForPaidInvoice()` is idempotent via `firstOrCreate(rme_invoice_item_id)`. Post-commit hook in `RmePaymentService::pay()` calls generation after payment transaction commits; generation failure logs warning and does NOT roll back payment. Branch isolation: service validates `invoice.branch_id === BranchContext::id()`. No real `LabOrder` created — candidates await Admin Lab review. 11 tests in `tests/Feature/RME/LabIntegrationTest.php`. Full suite: 1853 passed. Sprint 20 full-payment-only rule preserved.

## graphify

This project has a knowledge graph at graphify-out/ with god nodes, community structure, and cross-file relationships.

Rules:
- For codebase questions, first run `graphify query "<question>"` when graphify-out/graph.json exists. Use `graphify path "<A>" "<B>"` for relationships and `graphify explain "<concept>"` for focused concepts. These return a scoped subgraph, usually much smaller than GRAPH_REPORT.md or raw grep output.
- If graphify-out/wiki/index.md exists, use it for broad navigation instead of raw source browsing.
- Read graphify-out/GRAPH_REPORT.md only for broad architecture review or when query/path/explain do not surface enough context.
- After modifying code, run `graphify update .` to keep the graph current (AST-only, no API cost).

## Active Planning — Sprint 60 RME Multi-Page Handwriting Canvas

* Spec file: `docs/sprint_60_rme_multi_page_handwriting_canvas_spec.md`
* Status: **SPEC / PLANNING ONLY — not implemented yet.**
* Core rule: **1 canvas = 1 RM page.**
* One `MedicalRecord` can have multiple RM pages/canvases.
* Page 1 uses **locked read-through** backward compatibility from legacy `trx_medical_record_handwritings.handwriting_path`.
* Do **not** backfill legacy Page 1 during initial implementation.
* New additive table (`trx_medical_record_handwriting_pages`) is intended for **Page 2+**.
* Editor UX: read-only page preview → click/touch → same-page overlay editor → save → preview updates.
* KTP must remain hidden.
* Do **not** target `main`; continue using base branch: `feature/sprint-26-phase-26-8-stabilization-closure-go-watch-no-go-report`.

**Hotfix Sprint 60.8 — RME Room Assignment Gate Before Doctor Examination (2026-06-26):** Branch `hotfix/rme-room-assignment-gate-before-exam` (base `feature/sprint-26-phase-26-8-stabilization-closure-go-watch-no-go-report`; do NOT target main). Baseline before: Sprint 60.7 merge `b24f7d7`. Workflow: Pendaftaran → Antrian → **Input Ruangan** → diperiksa dokter → Consent → Kasir. Room assignment is a **queue-stage** requirement, not registration-stage. **No migration** — `clinic_room_id` (nullable FK, indexed) already exists on `trx_clinic_visits` since Sprint 58.6. Changes: (1) `ClinicVisit` adds `PRE_EXAM_STATUSES` (registered/waiting/in_progress), `hasAssignedRoom()`, `isTerminal()`, `requiresRoomBeforeExam()` (roomless AND pre-exam — cashier_pending & terminal are EXEMPT so Sprint 59 post-exam editing of finalized/old visits is never blocked). (2) New route-middleware `App\Modules\ClinicVisit\Middleware\EnsureVisitRoomAssigned` (alias `visit.room` in `bootstrap/app.php`) gates RM + Odontogram routes (`rme.visits.medical-record.show|store|update|finalize|handwriting`, `rme.visits.odontogram.show`, `rme.odontograms.update|finalize`); a roomless active visit redirects to `rme.visits.show` with flash `error` = "Pasien belum ditempatkan ke ruangan perawatan." — enforced at controller/middleware level, not just hidden buttons. (3) Reuses existing `rme.visits.assign-room` route/`assignRoom()`; service now rejects room reassignment on terminal visits and adds `activeRoomsForBranch()` (visit-branch-scoped, MAIN never selectable). (4) UI: visit detail shows "Menunggu Penempatan Ruangan" banner + inline branch-scoped room selector and locks RM/Odontogram buttons when roomless; patient-queue adds the waiting badge; cashier handoff (Sprint 60.7) shows room name or "Menunggu Ruangan / Belum siap diperiksa" (repo eager-loads `clinicRoom`). KTP stays hidden. **Test-fixture change:** `ClinicVisitFactory` now defaults `clinic_room_id` to a `ClinicRoom::factory()` (roomless tests pass explicit `null`); `ClinicRoomFactory` dropped `unique()` on its 5-name pool (random suffix already unique) so per-visit room creation does not overflow. Production registration (`ClinicVisitService::create`) still leaves new visits roomless. No payment/consent/invoice/receivable/SOAP changes; full-payment-only preserved; no JS/CSS asset change (no `npm run build` needed). Tests: new `tests/Feature/RME/RmeRoomAssignmentGateTest.php` (17). Green: RME dir 736, ClinicVisit 81, MedicalRecord 121, Odontogram 144, ClinicRoom master 14, DoctorCashierSyncQueue 15; pint + `git diff --check` clean.

## Sprint 62.1 — RME Doctor-to-Cashier Completion Gate (2026-06-27)

Branch `feature/sprint-62-1-rme-doctor-cashier-completion-gate` (base `feature/sprint-26-phase-26-8-stabilization-closure-go-watch-no-go-report`, HEAD `5ccf253`; do NOT target main). Enforces the RME pipeline `Pendaftaran → Antrian → Input Ruangan → Pemeriksaan Dokter → Consent → Selesai Pemeriksaan (cashier_pending) → Kasir / Pembayaran → Selesai Visit (completed)`. **No new status, no migration, no permission, no route.** Key rule: the doctor/front office can only advance a visit to `cashier_pending` ("Selesai Pemeriksaan"); `completed` ("Selesai Visit") is reachable ONLY from `cashier_pending` and in practice only via `RmePaymentService` after the invoice is settled. Changes: (1) `ClinicVisit::VALID_TRANSITIONS` removes the `in_progress → completed` edge (now `in_progress → {cashier_pending, cancelled}`). (2) `TransitionStatusRequest` drops `completed`, adds `cashier_pending`. (3) `ClinicVisitService::transitionStatus()` guards `→ completed` from any non-`cashier_pending` status with "Visit belum dapat diselesaikan total karena pembayaran belum selesai." (4) `RmeInvoiceService::create()` billing gate message → "Pembayaran belum dapat diproses karena pemeriksaan dokter belum selesai." (5) `ClinicVisitController` flashes "Pemeriksaan selesai, pasien masuk ke kasir." on the cashier handoff; `RmePaymentController` flashes "Pembayaran selesai, visit selesai total." on full payment. (6) `resources/views/rme/visits/show.blade.php` labels `cashier_pending` as "Selesai Pemeriksaan" and no longer renders a manual `completed` button. Consent gate (`CreateRmePaymentRequest`), room gate (`visit.room`), full-payment-only, SOAP-hidden, and KTP/scanned-doc/raw-note privacy all unchanged. Supervisor RME can access but cannot bypass gates (server-side). Owner KPI dashboard read-only and unchanged. Tests: new `tests/Feature/RME/RmeDoctorCashierCompletionGateTest.php` (14); updated `ClinicVisitTest` (3 old-workflow transition tests). Green: RME dir 801, ClinicVisit/CashierBilling/MedicalRecord/RmePayment regression 322, OwnerKpi+SupervisorRme+Permission 223; pint + `git diff --check` clean.

## Hotfix — RME Partial Payment Completes Visit (2026-06-27)

Branch `hotfix/rme-partial-payment-completes-visit` (base `feature/sprint-26-phase-26-8-stabilization-closure-go-watch-no-go-report`, HEAD `3ced441`; do NOT target main). **Corrects** the Sprint 62.1 partial-payment rule: **bayar sebagian tetap menyelesaikan visit**. Any successful cashier payment (`amount > 0`) now completes the current visit; the remaining balance stays active piutang and is collected at a future follow-up, instead of holding the old visit open at `cashier_pending`. **No status/migration/permission/route added.** Mapping: no payment → invoice `UNPAID`, visit `cashier_pending`; partial → invoice `PARTIAL`, visit `completed`, remaining = active piutang; full → invoice `PAID`, visit `completed`, no piutang; zero-grand-total/free control → unchanged (settles on `remainingAmount() == 0`). Change: `RmePaymentService::completeVisitIfPaid()` → `completeVisitAfterCashierPayment()` transitions `cashier_pending → completed` on `PAID` **or** `PARTIAL` (still guarded by `status === cashier_pending`); partial flash → "Pembayaran sebagian berhasil, visit selesai dan sisa tagihan masuk piutang." Sprint 62.1 gates preserved — completion runs only inside `pay()` after payable/consent/room asserts; doctor still cannot mark `completed` directly (`in_progress → completed` edge stays removed); Supervisor RME cannot bypass. Receivable list + Owner KPI key off **invoice** status/remaining (not visit status), so completed partial visits still show as piutang; zero-grand-total/zero-remaining stay excluded. Tests: `RmeDoctorCashierCompletionGateTest` (15), updated stale partial assertions in `RmePaymentTest` + `RmeControlVisitReceivableCarryOverPaymentTest`. Green: RME dir 802, CashierBilling 33, RmePayment 19, OwnerKpiDashboard+Receivable 129, SupervisorRme+Permission 212; pint + `git diff --check` clean.

## Sprint 62.2 — Patient Outstanding Receivable Carry-over for New Visits (2026-06-27)

Branch `feature/sprint-62-2-patient-outstanding-receivable-carryover` (base `feature/sprint-26-phase-26-8-stabilization-closure-go-watch-no-go-report`, HEAD `150d2d2`; do NOT target main). Spec: `docs/sprint_62_2_patient_outstanding_receivable_carryover_spec.md`. Lets the cashier see + **optionally** collect a patient's previous outstanding receivables during **any** new RME visit — **without merging old debt into the new invoice** (each `trx_rme_invoice` keeps its own status/ledger/grand_total). **No migration, no permission, no route, no table/column.** New on `RmeControlReceivableService`: `getOutstandingReceivablesForPatientVisit(visit, currentInvoice)` (patient's other UNPAID/PARTIAL invoices, RME branches, grand_total>0, remaining>0, !=current, oldest-first by visit_date,id) + `getVisitPayableSummary(currentInvoice, selectedIds[])` whose `selected_receivables` is always the **server-side intersection** of eligible ∩ submitted ids (the IDOR boundary — forged/other-patient/other-branch/paid ids are dropped). New `RmePaymentService::allocateVisitPayment()` (sibling of `allocateControlPayment`, reuses shared private helpers): single `DB::transaction`+`lockForUpdate`, FIFO selected prior receivables (oldest-first, branch+payable re-asserted under lock) → current invoice, one `trx_rme_payment` per real invoice id under a shared `payment_batch_uuid`. New `completeVisitAfterCashierBatch()` generalizes the partial-payment hotfix: any payment in the batch completes the current `cashier_pending` visit, unfilled balances stay active piutang. **Control-chain path (`getControlPayableSummary`/`allocateControlPayment`) deliberately UNCHANGED** (control keeps its stricter "own invoice must settle" completion; ordinary path uses "any payment completes"). Controller `create()` adds `outstandingReceivables` (ordinary only); `store()` routes to `allocateVisitPayment` when server-recomputed `selected_remaining>0`, else plain `pay()`/`allocateControlPayment`. `CreateRmePaymentRequest` adds nullable `selected_receivable_ids[]` and caps amount at `total_payable` when receivables selected. UI: opt-in "Piutang Sebelumnya" card in `payment/create.blade.php`, **unchecked by default**, Alpine live total, **no KTP/NIK/scans/raw notes**. Owner KPI/receivable report unchanged (per-invoice remaining, counted once, branch-attributed). Sprint 62.1 doctor→cashier + consent + room + branch gates preserved server-side. Tests: new `tests/Feature/RME/PatientOutstandingReceivableCarryOverTest.php` (20). Advisory columns (Ruangan, Tindakan Awal, Keluhan Utama, TTD Dokter/Pasien) are **staged only — never promoted to RME**. **Migrations (additive, `migrate` only — never `migrate:fresh`/`db:wipe`):** `stg_legacy_patient_import_batches`, `stg_legacy_patient_imports`, nullable indexed `mst_patients.import_batch_id` FK, and **`mst_patients.doctor_id` relaxed to nullable** (unresolved legacy doctor → `doctor_id = NULL`; mirrors Sprint 23.9.1 `clinic_id` relaxation; non-destructive). New `App\Modules\Patient\Models\LegacyPatientImportBatch` (status `uploaded|validated|committing|committed|rolled_back|failed`) + `LegacyPatientImportRow` (status `valid|warning|error|committed|skipped|rolled_back`); `App\Modules\Patient\Services\LegacyPatientImportService` (`parseAndStage`/`validateAndMapRow`/`commit`/`rollback`/`discard`/`templateRows`/`errorReportRows`); `LegacyPatientImportController` + `UploadLegacyPatientCsvRequest`. Commit = single `DB::transaction` + `lockForUpdate` RM/KTP re-check (preview→commit race → row `skipped`, never overwritten); idempotent (committed batch re-commit = no-op). Rollback soft-deletes the batch's patients (`import_batch_id` + `committed_patient_id`), **blocked when any imported patient has a downstream visit/RM**; pre-commit discard soft-deletes staging only. **KTP/NIK masked everywhere** (`maskKtp`) — full value only in JSON payload columns, never rendered/exported. clinic_id left NULL (branch-anchored). Routes `settings.patients.import.{index,template,store,show,errors,commit,rollback,destroy}` under existing `manage patients` group (**no new permission**); views `settings/patients/import/{create,preview}`; sidebar "Impor Pasien Legacy". MAIN excluded; branch set = active + RME-enabled. No Sprint 62.1/62.2 payment/receivable logic changed; no RME gate weakened. Tests: new `tests/Feature/Patient/LegacyPatientBatchImportTest.php` (24). ## LB-1 — Load Balancer Pilot (2026-07-05)

Branch `feature/lb-1-load-balancer-pilot` (base `feature/sprint-26-phase-26-8-stabilization-closure-go-watch-no-go-report`; PR #181 squash-merged, merge commit `1be6f50`). GO tag `lb-1-load-balancer-pilot-go` @ `1be6f50`. Deployed to VPS pilot; smoke PASS. Foundation/readiness-only — **no real multi-node traffic switch, no runtime driver forced, no file moved, no migration**. New `config/load_balancer.php` reads `LB_*` env (all added to `.env.example` safe/no-secret, `LB_TRUSTED_PROXIES=` empty by default). `bootstrap/app.php` wires an **opt-in** `$middleware->trustProxies(at: ...)` from `LB_TRUSTED_PROXIES` — skipped entirely when empty, so Laravel's stock `TrustProxies` (already in the framework's default global middleware stack) behavior is untouched by default. New minimal unauthenticated route `GET|HEAD /health/lb` (`health.lb` → `App\Http\Controllers\LoadBalancerHealthController`) always returns `{"status":"ok","service":"daengtisiams","check":"lb"}` (or `"fail"`/503 if the optional `LB_HEALTH_DEEP_CHECK` DB ping fails) — never APP_KEY/DB host/env/route list/user data/KTP/NIK/stack traces; disabled via `LB_HEALTH_ENDPOINT_ENABLED=false` (route absent, 404). New `App\Support\LoadBalancer\LoadBalancerReadinessService` (read-only): reports trusted-proxy config, forwarded-header/HTTPS/secure-cookie expectations vs actual `APP_URL` scheme/`session.secure`, session/cache/queue drivers, STORAGE-1 object storage enabled flag, STATELESS-1 runtime readiness status; also exposes `headerSmoke()` which simulates forwarded-header resolution **in-process** (synthetic proxy IP added to a copy of the trusted list for the simulation only — no real network call, no config mutation), skipped with a reason when `LB_TRUSTED_PROXIES` is empty. Command `php artisan lb:readiness-check` (`--json`, `--strict`/`--fail-on-warning`, `--header-smoke`, `--url=` reserved). Status enum `single_vps_ready|lb_pilot_ready|warning|fail`; "multi-node expected" signal is `LB_STRICT`, not merely `LB_EXPECT_FORWARDED_HEADERS` (a single VPS behind nginx already expects forwarded headers without running multiple instances). New `App\Services\Foundation\LoadBalancerGovernanceService` publishes **LB-R001..R010** (explicit trusted proxy required, no raw-IP trust without forwarded-header consideration, minimal/non-sensitive health endpoint, HTTPS/proxy verified before secure-cookie changes, shared session/cache/queue for real multi-node, non-destructive smoke, explicit cache rebuild/rollback, request-id roadmap before big multi-node traffic, governance summary shows LB without weakening other chains, no public diagnostic endpoint exposing internal state) into `architecture:foundation-governance-summary` as an informational `lb_governance` section (not wired into the blocking `combinedDecision`; `storage_governance`/`stateless_governance` unchanged). `config/foundation_roadmap.php` LB-1 marked `completed` (next recommended: `REPLICA-1`); updated 5 existing roadmap-assertion tests accordingly (`FoundationRoadmapGovernanceTest`, `Nsf9ReleaseSafetyGovernanceTest`, `Dbperf2GovernanceIntegrationTest`, `Storage1GovernanceIntegrationTest`, `Stateless1GovernanceIntegrationTest`). Docs: `docs/architecture/load-balancer-pilot-readiness.md`, `docs/architecture/load-balancer-governance-rules.md`. Tests: `Lb1GovernanceIntegrationTest` (7), `LoadBalancerReadinessCheckCommandTest` (8), `HealthLbEndpointTest` (4). Full suite green: 4019 passed. VPS: pre-deploy backup `pre_lb_1_load_balancer_pilot_20260705-085410.sql` (623KB); on VPS `lb:readiness-check` → `single_vps_ready`/`GO` (trusted proxies not configured, as expected for this pilot); `/health/lb` returns the minimal JSON with and without forwarded headers (no trusted proxy configured, so headers are ignored by design); `storage_governance`/`stateless_governance`/`lb_governance` all `GO`; roadmap `next_recommended_sprint` = `REPLICA-1`; no new Laravel log errors from this deploy.

## OBS-1 — Request ID, Correlation ID & Observability Foundation (2026-07-05)

Branch `feature/obs-1-request-id-correlation-observability-foundation` (base `feature/sprint-26-phase-26-8-stabilization-closure-go-watch-no-go-report`; PR #184 squash-merged, merge commit `df07d90`). New `App\Http\Middleware\AttachRequestCorrelationContext` registered globally on the `web` middleware group (prepended in `bootstrap/app.php`): generates/validates a bounded `request_id`/`correlation_id` (inbound `X-Request-ID`/`X-Correlation-ID` only trusted when the matching trust flag is on **and** the value passes strict length/character validation — otherwise replaced with a generated UUID), attaches a minimal PII-free log context via `Log::withContext()` (request_id, correlation_id, method, path, route — each individually toggleable; user_id/branch_id off by default), and sets the `X-Request-ID` response header. New `App\Support\Observability\ObservabilityReadinessService` (read-only; detects middleware registration by resolving `Illuminate\Contracts\Http\Kernel` first — needed because Artisan/console context never builds the router's `web` middleware group otherwise) + command `php artisan obs:readiness-check` (`--json`, `--strict`/`--fail-on-warning`, `--header-smoke` — in-process simulation, no real network call). New `App\Services\Foundation\ObservabilityGovernanceService` publishes **OBS-R001..OBS-R012** (bounded request/correlation id, inbound trust only with strict validation, PII-free log context, sensitive-data masking, non-destructive readiness command, minimal health endpoints, multi-node/queue propagation roadmap, no PII to external vendors without review, prod debug off, governance summary non-regression, PII/secret masking statement required for future logging) into `architecture:foundation-governance-summary` as an informational `observability_governance` section (not wired into the blocking `combinedDecision`; `storage_governance`/`stateless_governance`/`lb_governance`/`database_replica_governance`/`cache_redis_governance`/`cache_governance` unchanged). `config/foundation_roadmap.php` **not touched** — OBS-1 is not yet a tracked roadmap entry (roadmap `next_recommended_sprint` stays `REPLICA-1`); this is intentional, not an oversight. Docs: `docs/architecture/request-correlation-observability-readiness.md`, `docs/architecture/observability-governance-rules.md`. Tests: `Obs1GovernanceIntegrationTest` (5), `ObservabilityReadinessCheckCommandTest` (7), `RequestCorrelationMiddlewareTest` (7). Full suite green: 4066 passed, 11 skipped. VPS: pre-deploy backup `pre_obs_1_request_correlation_observability_20260705-114402.sql` (626KB); on VPS `obs:readiness-check` → `observability_ready`/GO (middleware detected, no warnings); an invalid/overlong inbound `X-Request-ID` against `/health/lb` does not break the request and a fresh generated id is returned instead; `storage_governance`/`stateless_governance`/`lb_governance`/`database_replica_governance`/`cache_redis_governance`/`cache_governance`/`observability_governance` all `GO`; no new Laravel log errors from this deploy.

## ROADMAP-1 — Foundation Roadmap Canonicalization (2026-07-05)

Branch `feature/roadmap-1-foundation-roadmap-canonicalization` (base `feature/sprint-26-phase-26-8-stabilization-closure-go-watch-no-go-report`, HEAD before this sprint `5cb632c`). Governance/config/docs/test only — **no runtime driver change, no migration, no business workflow change, no new external dependency**. **Important naming note:** `config/foundation_roadmap.php` already existed as a much older, source-locked "National Foundation Expansion Roadmap" (its own `roadmap_id` is literally `'ROADMAP-1'`, tag `roadmap-1-national-foundation-expansion-source-lock-go`, predating STORAGE-1) — this sprint does **not** replace that file/service/command; it fixes staleness inside it and adds a new, explicitly disambiguated `ROADMAP-1-CANONICALIZATION` entry (`disambiguates: 'ROADMAP-1'`) so the two are never conflated. Root cause fixed: `REPLICA-1`'s entry was still `status: 'planned'` even though it shipped and was GO-tagged, which made the existing `architecture:foundation-roadmap-check`'s `next_recommended_sprint` stale at `REPLICA-1`. Changes to `config/foundation_roadmap.php`: `REPLICA-1` → `completed` (+ `governance_section`/`readiness_command`/`go_tag`/`go_commit` evidence fields, also added retroactively to `STORAGE-1`/`STATELESS-1`/`LB-1`); five new `approved_sequence` entries inserted at priority 12–16 — `CACHE-1-REDIS-READINESS` (completed, disambiguated from the older priority-3 `CACHE-1` cache-strategy/invalidation-governance entry via `disambiguates`/`disambiguation_note` on both sides), `OBS-1` (completed), `OBS-2` (completed), `ROADMAP-1-CANONICALIZATION` (completed, this sprint), `MON-1` — *Health Monitoring, Alerting & Uptime Readiness* (planned, the new next sprint); `PART-1`/`SEARCH-1`/`NDA-1`/`RC-1` renumbered to priority 17–20 (metadata-only, `RC-1.depends_on` extended to include the five new ids). `next_recommended_sprint` now resolves to `MON-1`, never a completed sprint. New `App\Services\Foundation\FoundationRoadmapGovernanceService` publishes **ROADMAP-R001..R010** (every GO-tagged/deployed sprint must be in the roadmap; next sprint never stale; completed+governance-tracked entries need `go_tag` evidence; duplicate/similar names must be disambiguated; new-rule sprints register a governance section; roadmap check stays non-destructive; roadmap is not the sole source of truth over code/tests; roadmap updates stay governance/config/docs only; deploy evidence needs tag/commit/backup/smoke/rollback; canonicalization must never regress sibling foundation governance) into `architecture:foundation-governance-summary` as a new, additive `roadmap_governance` section (existing `roadmap` section/tests, backed by the untouched `App\Services\Architecture\FoundationRoadmapService` and `architecture:foundation-roadmap-check`, are unchanged) — informational only, not wired into the blocking `combinedDecision`. New thin command `php artisan foundation:roadmap-check` (`--json`, `--strict`, `--fail-on-warning`) wraps the existing `FoundationRoadmapService`/`FoundationRoadmapGovernanceService` (no duplicate validator) and reports `completed_sprints`/`current_sprint`/`next_recommended_sprint`/`stale_next_detected`/`missing_metadata`/`governance_sections_expected`/`governance_sections_known`/`warnings`/`recommendations` — read-only, no network/GitHub API call. Updated 5 existing tests that hardcoded the stale `next_recommended_sprint === 'REPLICA-1'` expectation (`FoundationRoadmapGovernanceTest`, `Storage1GovernanceIntegrationTest`, `Stateless1GovernanceIntegrationTest`, `Lb1GovernanceIntegrationTest`, `Dbperf2GovernanceIntegrationTest`, `Nsf9ReleaseSafetyGovernanceTest`) — the same kind of update every prior foundation sprint (STORAGE-1/STATELESS-1/LB-1) also made. Docs: `docs/architecture/foundation-roadmap-canonicalization.md`, `docs/architecture/foundation-roadmap-governance-rules.md`. Tests: new `FoundationRoadmapCanonicalizationTest` (12). Full suite green: 4096 passed, 11 skipped; pint + `git diff --check` clean. Next recommended sprint after ROADMAP-1: **`MON-1` — Health Monitoring, Alerting & Uptime Readiness**.

## ENT-5 — Queue, Retry & Failed Job Governance (2026-07-06)

Branch `feature/ent-5-queue-retry-failed-job-governance` (base `feature/sprint-26-phase-26-8-stabilization-closure-go-watch-no-go-report`). **Implementation-heavy** (first ENT sprint with real runtime code): operationalizes QUEUE-1 with an enforced retry/failed-job standard — **no migration (jobs/failed_jobs tables already exist), no route, no permission, no queue driver change, no worker started, no Horizon/Redis/vendor added**. New config section `config/queue_governance.php` → `ent5_retry_failed_job` (retry standards tries=3/backoff=[10,60,180]/timeout=120, caps 5/600; per-environment connection policy — sync local/testing only, pilot/staging/production = database|redis; failed driver `database-uuids` + `failed_jobs` table; allowed queue names default/reports/notifications/maintenance; unsafe-command scan patterns; worker runbook pointer; critical idempotency domains). New runtime `App\Support\Queue\EnterpriseQueueRetryDefaults` (trait: tries/backoff/timeout + `applyEnterpriseQueueRetryDefaults()`) and abstract base job `App\Support\Queue\EnterpriseQueueJob` — every future ShouldQueue class must extend it, use the trait, or declare explicit tries+backoff+timeout (**ENT5-Q008**). New read-only `App\Support\Queue\QueueRetryFailedJobReadinessService` (connection-vs-environment policy, failed storage, retry-standard sanity, static ShouldQueue compliance scan, destructive-command scan with patterns kept in config so app/ never contains the literals, runbook presence) + command `php artisan foundation:queue-retry-failed-job-check` (`--json`, `--strict`/`--fail-on-warning`; FAIL→1, WATCH→1 only under strict). New `App\Services\Foundation\QueueRetryFailedJobGovernanceService` publishes **ENT5-Q001..Q008** into `architecture:foundation-governance-summary` as informational `queue_retry_governance` section (QUEUE-1's `queue_governance` section untouched — same separation pattern as cache_governance vs cache_redis_governance; not wired into blocking combinedDecision). Roadmap: ENT-4 gains `deploy_evidence_commit` `321c05d`; ENT-5 → `completed` with `governance_section`/`readiness_command`/`policy_doc`/`worker_runbook_doc`/`go_tag` `ent-5-queue-retry-failed-job-governance-go`; rules gain `queue_retry_failed_job_governance_locked` + doc pointers; `next_recommended_sprint` → **ENT-6**. Docs: `docs/architecture/queue-retry-failed-job-governance.md` (durable lock, referenced from freeze-rules Section 7), `docs/architecture/queue-worker-operations-runbook.md` (worker/failed-job ops — manual only, deploy never starts workers; supervisor template for future rollout), `docs/sprints/ent-5-queue-retry-failed-job-governance.md`; AI mirror rule `.cursor/rules/54-queue-retry-failed-job-governance.mdc`. VPS posture: **worker-ready, not worker rollout** — no systemd/supervisor worker enabled (zero ShouldQueue classes exist yet). Tests: new `Ent5QueueRetryFailedJobGovernanceTest` + `QueueRetryFailedJobGovernanceCommandTest` (includes real end-to-end: fixture job extending `EnterpriseQueueJob` dispatched to database queue, processed via `queue:work --once`, failure lands in `failed_jobs` and is retried/forgotten per uuid); updated the sibling tests pinning next=ENT-5 → ENT-6.

Local + CI green (new tests 24 passed/230 assertions, Architecture+Foundation 401 passed, full suite 4177 passed/11 skipped/19562 assertions, pint + `git diff --check` clean; CI run `28761577594` required gates pass, full-suite CI gate skipped while local full suite passed). **VPS pilot deployed** (VPS was previously at ENT-4 merge `9c15db2`; this deploy also carried the ENT-4 deploy-evidence commit `321c05d`): pre-deploy backup `pre_auto_deploy_20260706-011910.sql` (618K) via `scripts/deploy-vps.sh`; `Nothing to migrate.` as expected; all deploy governance gates GO; automated smoke 7/7 GO incl. Branch `feature/ent-7-developer-assistance-console` (base `feature/sprint-26-phase-26-8-stabilization-closure-go-watch-no-go-report`). **Implementation-heavy**: real read-only diagnostic console for Super Admin at `GET /dev-console` (route `developer-console.index`), gated by new permission `view_developer_console` (PermissionSeeder; Super Admin passes via `Gate::before`), every page view audited to `sys_audit_logs` (`VIEW_DEVELOPER_CONSOLE`) + app log with OBS-1 correlation context — **no migration, no runtime driver change, no vendor, no worker, no RME/payment/inventory workflow change**. Panels: runtime health (DB ping, cache/session/queue drivers, pending/failed jobs, object storage flag, env/debug), storage writable paths (STATELESS-1), disk+backup files, failed_jobs summary (ENT-5), sys_audit_logs metadata (no payloads), NSF-3 pg_stat_statements slow-query summary, release-evidence artifacts + last governance decision, masked application log tail. All free text passes `App\Support\DeveloperConsole\SensitiveValueMasker` (13+-digit runs, credentials/tokens/authorization, emails). New `config/developer_console.php`; `App\Support\DeveloperConsole\DeveloperConsoleService` (per-panel try/catch → "unavailable"); thin `DeveloperConsoleController`; view `dev-console/index.blade.php`; sidebar item. Governance: `App\Services\Foundation\DeveloperConsoleGovernanceService` publishes **ENT7-DC001..DC010** into `architecture:foundation-governance-summary` as informational `developer_console_governance` (queue_retry_governance/idempotency_outbox_governance untouched; not in blocking combinedDecision); command `php artisan foundation:developer-console-check` (`--json`, `--strict`/`--fail-on-warning`) re-verifies ENT-5 + ENT-6 GO, route GET-only posture, permission gating, audit + masking config. Evidence: `developer-console-check.json` required in CI+VPS release-evidence profiles, generated by CI gates + `scripts/deploy-vps.sh`. Roadmap: ENT-6 gains `go_commit` `46f41e8893a1892b9c725daefbf43d41ced967cd`; ENT-7 → `completed` with governance_section/readiness_command/policy_doc/go_tag `ent-7-developer-assistance-console-go`; rules gain `developer_console_governance_locked` + doc pointer; `next_recommended_sprint` → **ENT-8**. Docs: `docs/architecture/developer-assistance-console-governance.md` (durable lock, referenced from freeze-rules Section 8), `docs/sprints/ent-7-developer-assistance-console.md`; AI mirror `.cursor/rules/56-developer-assistance-console.mdc`. VPS note: run `php artisan db:seed --class=PermissionSeeder --force` once post-deploy (idempotent) to register the permission. Tests: `Ent7DeveloperAssistanceConsoleTest`, `DeveloperConsoleGovernanceCommandTest`, `DeveloperConsoleTest`; sibling tests pinning next=ENT-7 updated to ENT-8.

New `config/deployment_rollback.php` (required markers + destructive-command patterns config-not-code + evidence/safety expectations). ## ENT-12 — Backup & Disaster Recovery Automation (2026-07-06)

Branch `feature/ent-12-backup-disaster-recovery-automation` (base `feature/sprint-26-phase-26-8-stabilization-closure-go-watch-no-go-report`). Scope source: `config/foundation_roadmap.php` ENT-12 entry (title *Backup & Disaster Recovery Automation*, category `backup_dr`, depends ENT-11/NSF-10). **Implementation-heavy**: automates DB backup + retention and a rehearsable, non-production restore drill on top of the NSF-10 `foundation:backup-verify` gate and the ENT-11 deploy/rollback automation — **no migration, no route, no permission, no queue driver change, no queue worker enabled, no business workflow change, no destructive DB command, KTP/NIK never exposed**. New runtime `scripts/backup-vps.sh` (fail-fast `set -euo pipefail`; `pg_dump` into `storage/app/backups/deploy` → `foundation:backup-verify` → prune dumps older than `BACKUP_DR_RETENTION_DAYS` keeping a `min_retained_backups` floor; no destructive DB command). New runtime `scripts/restore-rehearsal.sh` (fail-fast; selects latest verified backup → restores into a **scratch** database `REHEARSAL_DB` explicitly guarded to differ from the production DB (aborts if equal) → verifies restored table count → drops only the scratch DB → writes non-sensitive `restore-rehearsal.json` evidence with path/size/table-count/RTO/RPO; **never restores over production and never calls the production restore helper** — production data restore stays the explicit `scripts/restore_postgres.sh` step). New `config/backup_dr.php` (automation files, destructive-command patterns config-not-code, backup/rehearsal expectations, retention, RTO/RPO objectives, evidence artifact, pre-deploy gate). New read-only `App\Support\Backup\BackupDrScanner` (backup script, restore-rehearsal script, retention/RTO/RPO objectives, evidence profiles, release-safety posture). New `App\Services\Foundation\BackupDrGovernanceService` publishes **ENT12-BDR001..BDR012** into `architecture:foundation-governance-summary` as informational `backup_dr_governance` (not in blocking combinedDecision; ENT-5/6/7/8/9/10/11 sections untouched), re-verifies ENT-5+6+7+8+9+10+11 GO; command `php artisan foundation:backup-dr-check` (`--json`, `--strict`/`--fail-on-warning`). Evidence artifact `backup-dr-check.json` required in CI+VPS release-evidence profiles (`ReleaseEvidenceService` job map, `config/release_evidence.php`, `config/foundation_governance.php` ENT-12 CI-gate registry, CI workflow + `scripts/ci/foundation-evidence-gates.sh`), pre-deploy gate in `config/release_safety.php` (+ backup_script/restore_rehearsal_script in deploy_gate_files). `scripts/deploy-vps.sh` runs `foundation:backup-dr-check` + captures its evidence artifact after the ENT-11 gate (preserved ENT-8 cache-clear ordering; ENT-12 adds no route). Branch `feature/post-ent-foundation-runtime-hardening-queue-worker-deploy-timeout` (base `feature/sprint-26-phase-26-8-stabilization-closure-go-watch-no-go-report`). **Post-closure hardening / follow-up sprint — NOT ENT-17.** ENT-16 already closed ENT-1..ENT-16 with the final tag `enterprise-foundation-go`; this sprint inherits that closed baseline, adds no ENT roadmap entry, moves no GO tag, and leaves `next_recommended_sprint = MON-1`. GO tag `post-ent-foundation-runtime-hardening-queue-worker-deploy-timeout-go`. **No migration, no route, no permission, no runtime driver change, no destructive command, KTP/NIK never exposed.** Three governed, testable postures: (1) **ENT-1..ENT-4 audit** — canonical scope resolution: ENT-1 (architecture baseline lock), ENT-2 (DB performance contract), ENT-3 (reporting summary contract), ENT-4 (Redis cache policy) were governance/config/docs LOCKS, so **no runtime backfill was required or performed**; the audit verifies each is completed + GO-tagged + doc-backed (with rule-id markers) and FAILs on any real missing artifact. (2) **Queue worker runtime** — conservative single-process systemd unit `deploy/systemd/daengtisiams-queue-worker.service` (`artisan queue:work database --queue=default,reports,notifications,maintenance --sleep=3 --tries=3 --backoff=10,60,180 --timeout=120 --memory=256 --max-time=3600 --rest=1`, `Restart=always`, `User=www-data`); worker command only (never listener/legacy-daemon), approved queues ⊆ ENT-5, broker connection validated against the ENT-5 `environment_connection_policy` (sync allowed in local/testing, forbidden in pilot/prod); the deploy script NEVER starts the worker (ENT-5 `no_long_running_worker_started_by_deploy`) — operator activates it after green gates. Harmless `App\Jobs\Foundation\QueueWorkerSmokeJob` (extends `EnterpriseQueueJob`, logs one non-PII line) + `foundation:queue-worker-smoke`. (3) **Deploy evidence timeout hardening** — `scripts/deploy-vps-runner.sh` runs `scripts/deploy-vps.sh` DETACHED (`setsid`+`nohup`) with a log + status file so the slow VPS evidence phase survives an SSH broken pipe (the ENT-16 deploy issue); GO reported only on `exit=0`. New `config/enterprise_foundation_runtime_hardening.php`, `App\Support\Foundation\EntFoundationRuntimeHardeningScanner`, `App\Services\Foundation\EntFoundationRuntimeHardeningGovernanceService` (rules **PEH-R001..PEH-R012**), commands `foundation:ent-1-4-audit-check` / `foundation:queue-worker-runtime-check` / `foundation:runtime-hardening-check` (all `--json`/`--strict`). Informational `enterprise_foundation_runtime_hardening_governance` section in `architecture:foundation-governance-summary` (re-verifies ENT-5..16 GO; NOT in blocking combinedDecision). Optional ci/vps evidence artifacts `ent-1-4-audit-check.json` / `queue-worker-runtime-check.json` / `runtime-hardening-check.json` (captured by deploy + CI, non-blocking). Docs: `docs/architecture/post-enterprise-foundation-runtime-hardening-governance.md`, runbooks `docs/runbooks/queue-worker-activation-runbook.md` + `docs/runbooks/vps-deploy-evidence-timeout-recovery-runbook.md`, `docs/sprints/post-ent-foundation-runtime-hardening.md`, AI mirror `.cursor/rules/66-post-enterprise-foundation-runtime-hardening.mdc`, roadmap `rules` pointers. Tests: `tests/Feature/Architecture/PostEntFoundationRuntimeHardeningTest.php` (19). ENT-5..ENT-16 strict gates + enterprise-closure all stay GO; MON-1 remains next recommended. **VPS worker activation (post-deploy, operator-run):** `sudo cp deploy/systemd/daengtisiams-queue-worker.service /etc/systemd/system/ && sudo systemctl daemon-reload && sudo systemctl enable --now daengtisiams-queue-worker.service`.

## CICD-CTRL-1 — Safe CI Runtime Control & Required-Gate Optimization (2026-07-09)

Branch `feature/cicd-ctrl-1-safe-ci-runtime-control` (base `feature/sprint-26-phase-26-8-stabilization-closure-go-watch-no-go-report`; do NOT target main). **CI/CD control sprint (not a product feature sprint)** — makes normal PRs faster without letting any risky change bypass required quality/security/governance/smoke/release-safety gates. Runs after the closed UIX foundation (UIX-22 final GO). **No app runtime / route / policy / permission / query / data change; no schema/migration; no paid CI SaaS; no heavy dep.** Core deliverable: new conservative classifier `scripts/ci/resolve-gates.sh` (bash, `set -euo pipefail`, read-only — only `git diff --name-only`) that classifies the change set **DEFAULT-STRONG** into one of 7 profiles (strongest first: `unknown_high_risk` > `ci_workflow` > `dependency_or_build` > `permissions_security` > `runtime_app` > `ui_only` > `docs_only`) and emits parseable `key=value` (`gate_profile`, `run_critical_tests`, `run_{ui,permission,inventory,rme,lab}_tests`, `run_build`, `run_full_suite`) + a human safety summary to stderr; supports `--base/--head`, `--changed-files-file`, `--changed-files-stdin`, `--json`, `--github-output`. **Safety invariant: `docs_only` (every changed file is Markdown) is the ONLY profile that skips the expensive critical Pest step**; any uncertainty (no diff / unreachable base / unknown path) → `unknown_high_risk` → everything runs; workflow/script/dependency/test/app/config/migration/route/policy/permission changes always force the critical gate. Workflow `.github/workflows/foundation-evidence-gates.yml` gains a first `classify` job (outputs consumed via `needs.classify.outputs.*`); **always-on jobs `quality_gate` + `release_safety_gate` (NSF-9) + `nsf10_release_evidence_gate` are never gated**; `critical_test_gate` keeps migrate + governance-audit unconditional and gates only the `php artisan test --filter=...` step (`if run_critical_tests != 'false'`, else writes a skip-note evidence file); new parallel **additive** `selective_module_gate` runs Inventory/Lab/UI/Permission suites for changed modules (coverage PRs previously only got on push-to-base — never reduces critical); `full_suite_gate` preserved (schedule weekly / manual dispatch / push-to-base) and only its heavy step skips for a docs-only push. Anti-abuse: `paths-ignore`/blanket path filtering forbidden; high-risk profiles force all module tests + `run_full_suite=required`. Governance: `config/ci_runtime_control.php` (contract: profiles, `skip_critical_profiles=[docs_only]`, `default_profile=unknown_high_risk`, always-on jobs, required/forbidden workflow markers), `App\Support\Cicd\CiRuntimeControlScanner` (read-only classifier+workflow+invariant posture), `App\Services\Foundation\CiRuntimeControlGovernanceService` (rules **CICDCTRL-R001..R011**, re-verifies ENT-10 enterprise gate stays GO; informational, NOT in blocking combinedDecision), command `php artisan foundation:ci-runtime-control-check` (`--json`/`--strict`). Docs: `docs/sprints/cicd-ctrl-1-safe-ci-runtime-control.md`, `docs/runbooks/ci-runtime-control-runbook.md`, AI mirror `.cursor/rules/67-safe-ci-runtime-control.mdc`. Tests: new `tests/Feature/Cicd/SafeCiRuntimeControlTest.php` (14 — docs/app/route/migration/config/test/permission/workflow/dependency/UI/inventory/lab/unknown/mixed/empty; asserts high-risk never selects a weak gate). Green locally: CICD-CTRL 14; `foundation:ci-runtime-control-check --strict`/`cicd-enterprise-gate-check`/`enterprise-documentation-check`/`security-compliance-check`/`ui-governance-check --strict`/`roadmap-check --strict` (next `MON-1`, not stale) GO; `npm run build` pass; `pint --dirty --test` + `git diff --check` clean; `bash -n` clean; graphify updated. **Next product sprint: Inventory Sprint 68.45** (roadmap `next_recommended_sprint` is `MON-1` — foundation track — untouched by this control sprint; document any conflict, do not silently override).

## FIX-PRE-68-45 — RME/Owner/Doctor/Inventory-PR Polish Fix Sprint (2026-07-09)

Branch family `feature/fix-pre-68-45-rme-owner-doctor-inventory-pr-polish` (base `feature/sprint-26-phase-26-8-stabilization-closure-go-watch-no-go-report`; do NOT target main). Scoped pre-Inventory-68.45 fix sprint bundling 8 UX/reporting/inventory-workflow fixes (Scopes A–H), **phased into 3 PRs** so production risk on the live VPS pilot is isolated per deploy: PR #1 = A+D+B (view/dashboard polish), PR #2 = C+F (doctor report + vendor filters), PR #3 = E+G (GR default batch/lot + PR workflow). CICD-CTRL-1 classifier + all governance/security/release-safety/smoke gates preserved unchanged; ledger-only inventory, branch isolation, KTP/NIK privacy, full-payment-only, room gate, and doctor→cashier gate all preserved.

**Phase 1 (PR #1) — Scopes A, D, B (presentation + read-only reporting; no schema/permission/route change):**
- **Scope A — Rekam Medis UX** (`resources/views/rme/visits/medical-record/show.blade.php`): the plain-text `Kembali ke detail kunjungan` link is replaced by a real `x-ui.button variant="secondary"` "Kembali ke Kunjungan" at the **top** of the page (page-header actions slot), matching the Odontogram page. The "Buku RM Pasien" sheet nav (`rm-sheet-nav` partial) and "Riwayat Pencatatan" card are **relocated below** the full handwriting canvas so the doctor reaches the handwritten RME with less scrolling. No RM finalization / edit-after-final / room-gate / workspace-resolver logic changed — presentation only.
- **Scope D — Resep UX** (`resources/views/rme/visits/prescription/show.blade.php`): real "Kembali ke Kunjungan" button at the top actions area (Odontogram-style); the plain bottom link removed. The **handling doctor name** (`$clinicVisit->doctor?->name` — canonical `ClinicVisit belongsTo Doctor`) is shown under the signature canvas in edit mode ("Dokter yang menangani kunjungan ini") and under the saved-signature block in view mode. No prescription workflow/validation change.
- **Scope B — Owner dashboard charts + module shortcuts** (`app/Modules/Reporting/Services/OwnerDashboardKpiService.php`, `app/Http/Controllers/HomeDashboardController.php`, `resources/views/dashboards/owner-kpi.blade.php`): visit + payment **trend charts activated** — rendered server-side with the existing Tailwind stack (no React/Vue, no charting dep) using the already-existing `dailyVisitTrend`/`dailyPaymentTrend` branch-scoped data, now with peak/total captions, per-bar hover value labels, and a first/last date axis. New **permission-gated** `OwnerDashboardKpiService::moduleShortcuts(User)` returns module/report-category shortcuts (Owner summary always; RME patient report gated `view_rme_patient_reports`; RME payment report gated `view_rme_payment_reports`; Piutang gated `manage_rme_billing`; Inventory report gated `view_inventory|manage_inventory`) — each `Route::has`-guarded; a user only ever sees reports they may already open and the target report's own branch scope still applies (no cross-branch leak introduced here). Branch filter + cross-branch analytics behavior unchanged. Owner KPI formulas, privacy (no KTP/NIK/scans/raw notes), and gold-CTA-revenue-only rule preserved.
- Tests: new `tests/Feature/RME/FixPre6845Phase1UxTest.php` (5 — Scope A relocation + top back button, Scope D back button + handling-doctor name, Scope B permission-gated shortcuts + dashboard render). Green: FixPre6845Phase1Ux 5; MedicalRecord/Odontogram/Prescription/PatientCentricRmWorkspace/Sprint59/Sprint60 regression 340; Owner+Dashboard+OwnerDashboardUix 222; SafeCiRuntimeControl 14; `architecture:ui-governance-check --strict`/`foundation:ci-runtime-control-check --strict`/`security-compliance-check`/`cicd-enterprise-gate-check`/`enterprise-documentation-check`/`roadmap-check --strict` (next `MON-1`, not stale) all GO; `npm run build` pass; `pint --dirty --test` + `git diff --check` clean; graphify updated.
- **Shipped Phase 1:** PR #228 squash-merged `4e39d1d`; GO tag `fix-pre-68-45-phase-1-go` @ `4e39d1d` (exact-match on VPS). VPS pilot deployed via `scripts/deploy-vps-runner.sh` (backup `pre_auto_deploy_20260709-022946.sql`); automated smoke 7/7 GO; guest surfaces 200/302 (no 500); env=pilot debug=off; 0 new Laravel errors.

**Phase 2 (PR #2) — Scopes C, F (new doctor report + inventory vendor filters):**
- **Scope C — Doctor Performance / Income report** (`GET /rme/reports/doctor-performance` → `rme.reports.doctor-performance`, `DoctorPerformanceReportController@index`). New `App\Modules\RmeInvoice\Services\DoctorPerformanceReportService` aggregates RME invoice/payment truth scoped by the **visit's handling doctor** (`trx_clinic_visits.doctor_id`); "Total Dibayar" sums `trx_rme_payments.amount` for the doctor's visits (exact — payments are per-invoice/visit, no proportional allocation). **Two visibility tiers, IDOR-safe server-side:** `view_doctor_performance_report` (executive: all doctors + RME branches, filter by branch/doctor — Owner + Supervisor RME + Super Admin) shows a per-doctor summary or a one-doctor detail; `view_own_doctor_performance_report` + being a **linked doctor** (`mst_doctors.user_id`, Doctor role) is **forced to their own doctor_id** (a forged `?doctor_id=` is ignored); anything else → 403. Detail view = KPIs (kunjungan, pasien, ditagih, dibayar, sisa) + treatment breakdown (items, items-on-paid-invoice, billed subtotal). Filters: date range, branch (executive), doctor (executive), treatment, payment status. New permissions `view_doctor_performance_report` (Owner/Supervisor RME) + `view_own_doctor_performance_report` (Doctor) in PermissionSeeder/RoleSeeder; sidebar "Kinerja & Pendapatan Dokter" gated by either tier. Lab tables never mixed in; **no KTP/NIK**; no migration. Thin controller + `DoctorPerformanceReportRequest` + service (OwnerDashboardKpiService reporting-service precedent — queries models directly, no repository). Tests: `tests/Feature/RME/DoctorPerformanceReportTest.php` (6). **The Kepala-Cabang branch-scoped tier is deferred to Phase 3** (Scope G) where the Kepala Cabang role + user→branch link are actually created — avoids orphan infra.
- **Scope F — Inventory vendor/supplier filter + spend summary** (`inventory.reports.index`). New `supplier_id` filter (added to `InventoryReportFilterRequest` rules; branch-scoped drop in `InventoryReportService::sanitizeReportFilters` — a supplier not in the resolved branch is unset, IDOR boundary) applied via a null-guarded `->when($filters['supplier_id'], where('trx_inventory_movements.supplier_id', …))` on the current_stock/low_stock/mutation(period+opening)/valuation ledger builders (stock_card already supported it). Because only PO-linked Goods-Receipt PURCHASE movements carry `supplier_id`, the filter is **purchase-provenance** (documented in-view). New **per-vendor spend summary** ("Ringkasan Belanja per Vendor") sourced from **procurement truth** — `InventoryAnalyticsRepository::getSupplierPerformance($branchId)` (POSTED GR `line_total`), NOT the ledger — via new `InventoryReportService::supplierSpendSummary()` (try/catch → empty, degrades gracefully). New `reportSupplierOptions()` + `suppliers` in `getReportFilterOptions`; a branch-scoped `<select name="supplier_id">` shown only when vendor data exists. Ledger-only stock rule untouched; branch isolation preserved. Tests: `tests/Feature/Inventory/InventoryVendorFilterTest.php` (4). Green: Scope C 6, Scope F 4, inventory + RME report regression 380; `architecture:ui-governance-check --strict`/`foundation:ci-runtime-control-check --strict`/`security-compliance-check`/`cicd-enterprise-gate-check`/`enterprise-documentation-check`/`roadmap-check --strict` (next `MON-1`, not stale) all GO; `pint --dirty --test` + `git diff --check` clean; `npm run build` pass; graphify updated.

**Scope H (rules/docs) — durable rules from this sprint:** (1) The doctor performance report is **doctor-own-data only** for linked doctors (IDOR-forced server-side); executive tiers require an explicit named permission; the **Kepala-Cabang branch-scoped** doctor report is introduced in Phase 3 with the Kepala Cabang role + user→branch link. (2) Owner dashboard branch/module filters are **permission-safe** (each shortcut `Route::has` + `can()` gated; the target report's own branch scope still applies — no cross-branch leak). (3) Inventory **vendor report filters stay branch-safe** (supplier scoped to the resolved branch in `sanitizeReportFilters`) and the **vendor spend total sources procurement/PO/GR truth** (`getSupplierPerformance` POSTED GR `line_total`), never the stock ledger; the movement supplier filter is purchase-provenance. (These are enforced by the new tests; no new governance command was added to avoid false-positive rules.)
- **Shipped Phase 2:** PR #229 squash-merged `ceda3ba`; GO tag `fix-pre-68-45-phase-2-go` @ `ceda3ba` (VPS exact-match). VPS deployed via `scripts/deploy-vps-runner.sh` (backup `pre_auto_deploy_20260709-034027.sql`); permissions re-seeded (`db:seed --class=PermissionSeeder|RoleSeeder --force`, idempotent); smoke 7/7 GO; doctor-performance route 302 permission-safe; 0 new errors.

**Phase 3 (PR #3) — Scopes E, G (GR default batch/lot + branch PR workflow):**
- **Scope E — GR header-level default batch/lot** (`ValidatesGoodsReceiptInput`/`ValidatesGoodsReceiptBatchInput`, `_form.blade.php`). New optional GR-header inputs `default_batch_number` / `default_lot_number` / `default_batch_received_date` / `default_expiry_date` + checkbox `apply_default_batch_to_all` ("Terapkan batch/lot ini ke semua item yang wajib batch tracking"). `applyDefaultBatchToItems()` runs in `prepareForValidation` (before the existing per-item batch rules) and expands the default into every **batch-tracked** item that has **no explicit batch of its own** (explicit = existing batch / entered batch_number / explicit `auto_batch=true`); item-level batch always overrides; non-batch-tracked products keep their batch empty. Because the batch found-or-create key is `(branch_id, product_id, batch_number, lot_number)`, one shared header `batch_number` yields a **DISTINCT batch row per product** at post time — never one shared global batch. Ledger stays ledger-only; the movement's `inventory_batch_id` is per-product-correct; FEFO/expiry unaffected (default sets a real expiry). If a batch-tracked item has neither its own batch nor a usable default → the existing per-item rule blocks with a clear message. **No migration** (GR items already have batch columns). Tests: `tests/Feature/Inventory/GoodsReceiptDefaultBatchTest.php` (4); 164 GR regression green.
- **Scope G — branch PR workflow (Kepala Cabang → Admin Warehouse).** New read-only board `GET /inventory/purchase-requests/workflow` (`inventory.purchase-requests.workflow`, `PurchaseRequestWorkflowController@index`, registered BEFORE the PR resource so it isn't captured as `{purchaseRequest}`), gated by the existing `viewAny PurchaseRequest` policy, **branch-scoped** via `BranchContext` (`PurchaseRequestService::branchWorkflowBoard`). Two **additive** migrations (additive-only, `migrate` — never `migrate:fresh`): `pr_type` (nullable, Reguler/Darurat) on `trx_purchase_requests`; **`users.branch_id`** (nullable FK, nullOnDelete) so a branch-scoped role is pinned to one branch (BranchContext already `Schema::hasColumn`-guards it — NULL keeps the existing MAIN fallback, so no existing user's behavior changes). New **Kepala Cabang role** (RoleSeeder) with ONLY `view dashboard` + `view_inventory` + `view_purchase_request` + `manage_purchase_request` — deliberately NOT `manage_purchase_order`/`manage_inventory`/`manage master data`/`approve_*`, so `PurchaseOrderPolicy::create` (the single server-side chokepoint) denies PO creation by route, UI, and direct request. `PurchaseRequest` gains `PR_TYPE_REGULER`/`PR_TYPE_DARURAT`/`isEmergency()`; `pr_type` added to fillable + validated (`in:reguler,darurat`) + persisted in `createDraft`/`updateDraft` (`normalizePrType`). `User` gains `branch_id` fillable + `homeBranch()` (convenience only — BranchContext reads the column directly). PR create form gains a Reguler/Darurat select (defaults from `?pr_type=`); sidebar adds "Alur PR Cabang". Existing PR/PO/GR pages untouched. Tests: `tests/Feature/Inventory/BranchPurchaseRequestWorkflowTest.php` (6 — Kepala Cabang creates PR Reguler + Darurat, cannot create PO (route + policy), branch-isolated board, Admin Warehouse can process, unauthorized 403).
- **Scope H additions:** (4) GR-level default batch/lot **expands per product/item (distinct batch per product), never one shared global batch across products**. (5) **Kepala Cabang is PR-create-only** and can never create a PO (enforced by the role omitting PO/manage-inventory permissions → `PurchaseOrderPolicy::create` denies server-side); branch scoping via `users.branch_id` (BranchContext-guarded, additive). Green: Scope E 4, Scope G 6, procurement/branch/permission regression; gates GO; pint + `git diff --check` clean; `npm run build` pass; graphify updated. Deploy note: after merge run `php artisan migrate --force` + `db:seed --class=PermissionSeeder --force && db:seed --class=RoleSeeder --force` on VPS.

## HOTFIX — FIX-PRE-68-45 Doctor Performance 403 (2026-07-09)

Branch `hotfix/fix-pre-68-45-doctor-performance-403` (base `feature/sprint-26-phase-26-8-stabilization-closure-go-watch-no-go-report`; do NOT target main). Rollback baseline `fix-pre-68-45-rme-owner-doctor-inventory-pr-polish-go`. Fixes the opaque 403 on the Scope C **Doctor Performance / Income** report (`GET /rme/reports/doctor-performance`) for doctor accounts **not linked** to a doctor record via `mst_doctors.user_id`, without weakening any permission. Doc: `docs/sprints/hotfix-fix-pre-68-45-doctor-performance-403.md`. **No migration, no route, no permission change, no schema change; KTP/NIK never rendered; CICD-CTRL-1 gates unchanged; Inventory Sprint 68.45 NOT started.** Changes: (1) `DoctorPerformanceReportService::resolveAccess()` splits a new **`unlinked`** tier out of `denied` (has `view_own_doctor_performance_report` but no doctor-record link) — report aggregation unchanged. (2) `DoctorPerformanceReportController::index()` returns a **clear** 403 for `unlinked` (*"Akun dokter belum terhubung ke data dokter. Hubungi admin untuk menghubungkan user ke master dokter."*) and a plain 403 for `denied`. (3) New read-only, privacy-safe `DoctorPerformanceReportService::accessAudit()` + command **`php artisan rme:doctor-performance-access-audit`** (`--json`, `--strict` → exit `2` on anomaly) detecting: Doctor-role users with no `mst_doctors.user_id`, doctor records without `user_id`, own-permission holders without a link (the ones that hit the clear 403; excludes executive/Super Admin), and Kepala Cabang permission leakage (user- and role-level) + missing permissions. **Access decision (official):** linked Doctor → own-data-only IDOR-forced; unlinked Doctor → clear 403 (never other doctors' data, never auto-link, never name/email inference); Owner/Supervisor RME → executive; Super Admin → all-permission role sync; **Kepala Cabang → 403 (no doctor-report permission this hotfix; branch-scoped access deferred to a future explicit branch-isolated sprint)**; no permission → 403. **Role permissions verified already correct — no seeder change** (Doctor=`view_own_doctor_performance_report`, Owner+Supervisor RME=`view_doctor_performance_report`, Kepala Cabang=none; `RoleSeeder`/`PermissionSeeder` idempotent). Sidebar stays permission-gated (`@canany`); route stays protected server-side. **Test note:** the Sprint 66.0 global `EnsureRmeOnlineContext` middleware redirects any Doctor-role user without a selected online context (302, orthogonal to this hotfix), so the access test bypasses it via `withoutMiddleware(EnsureRmeOnlineContext::class)` to exercise the report's own access boundary. Tests: `tests/Feature/RME/DoctorPerformanceReportAccessTest.php` (7), `tests/Feature/RME/DoctorPerformanceAccessAuditCommandTest.php` (9); regression `DoctorPerformanceReportTest` (6) + `RmeReportFilter|SidebarPermissionVisibility|RolePermissionHardening|PilotRouteAuthorization` (34) green. GO tag on merge: `hotfix-fix-pre-68-45-doctor-performance-403-go`. Deploy: reseed permissions idempotently then run `rme:doctor-performance-access-audit`; report unlinked doctors to admin (do NOT auto-link).

## HOTFIX — FIX-PRE-68-45 Doctor Performance Treatment Date Table (2026-07-09)

Branch `hotfix/fix-pre-68-45-doctor-performance-treatment-date` (base `feature/sprint-26-phase-26-8-stabilization-closure-go-watch-no-go-report`; do NOT target main). Rollback baseline `hotfix-fix-pre-68-45-doctor-performance-403-go`. Adds an **auto-displayed `Tanggal Perawatan` daily table** to the Doctor Performance / Income report (`GET /rme/reports/doctor-performance`) — a doctor is **never forced to pick a treatment date first**; the table is derived from the already-scoped result and each date expands into its per-treatment breakdown. Doc: `docs/sprints/hotfix-fix-pre-68-45-doctor-performance-treatment-date.md`. **No migration, no route, no permission change, no schema change; presentation + read-only aggregation only; KTP/NIK never rendered; all `-403` access rules preserved verbatim; Inventory Sprint 68.45 NOT started.** Changes: (1) New private `DoctorPerformanceReportService::dailyBreakdown($scope, $treatmentId, $invoiceStatus)` groups the scoped result by **treatment date = canonical `trx_clinic_visits.visit_date`** (same field the date-range filter uses), grouped in PHP for PG/SQLite portability (mirrors `OwnerDashboardKpiService`). Each date row: distinct patients, distinct treatment types (`jenis perawatan`), total items (`total tindakan`), **exact** paid total for that date (RME `trx_rme_payments` truth), and a nested per-treatment breakdown (name, item count, distinct patients, **exact billed subtotal**). Payment is only attributable at the visit/invoice level, so per-treatment shows exact **billed** ("Nilai Ditagih") and the **date-level** "Total Dibayar" is the exact RME payment sum. (2) `report()` adds `daily_rows` to the **detail** payload (always computed — no date filter required) and `[]` to the summary payload; the daily table appears whenever a doctor is scoped (always for the linked-doctor own tier; for executives after drilling into one doctor). (3) View `resources/views/rme/reports/doctor-performance.blade.php` gains a "Rincian Harian per Tanggal Perawatan" card (`Tanggal Perawatan | Jumlah Pasien | Jumlah Jenis Perawatan | Total Tindakan | Total Dibayar | Aksi`) above the existing aggregate breakdown; each date row expands via Blade + Alpine (`x-data="{ open:false }"`, `x-cloak`) into `Jenis Perawatan | Jumlah Tindakan | Jumlah Pasien | Nilai Ditagih` + a date-total row; empty state when no treatments; **no React/Vue, no new dep**. **Durable rules:** (a) the Doctor Performance report must auto-display `Tanggal Perawatan` in the table; (b) users must never be forced to select a treatment date first; (c) each date's breakdown is derived from that doctor's scoped actual data; (d) Doctor users never see other doctors' dates/treatments (IDOR-forced via `mst_doctors.user_id`); (e) Kepala Cabang stays blocked until a future explicit branch-scoped sprint with tests; (f) payment totals come from RME invoice/payment truth (never lab); (g) no KTP/NIK in doctor performance reports. Tests: new `tests/Feature/RME/DoctorPerformanceTreatmentDateTableTest.php` (14). Green: new 14, `DoctorPerformanceReport|DoctorPerformanceAccessAudit` 22, RME `Report|DoctorPerformance|Cashier|Payment` 231; `rme:doctor-performance-access-audit --strict` OK (exit 0, permissions seeded); gates GO (`architecture:ui-governance-check --strict`/`foundation:ci-runtime-control-check --strict`/`security-compliance-check`/`cicd-enterprise-gate-check`/`enterprise-documentation-check`/`roadmap-check --strict` next `MON-1` not stale); `pint --dirty --test` + `git diff --check` clean; `npm run build` pass; graphify updated. GO tag on merge: `hotfix-fix-pre-68-45-doctor-performance-treatment-date-go`.

## SPRINT-68.45 — Inventory Procurement Workflow Stabilization, GR Batch Default Hardening & Vendor Report Governance (2026-07-09)

Branch `feature/sprint-68-45-inventory-procurement-workflow-stabilization-gr-batch-vendor-governance` (base `feature/sprint-26-phase-26-8-stabilization-closure-go-watch-no-go-report`, baseline `hotfix-fix-pre-68-45-doctor-performance-treatment-date-go` @ `882c7a6`; do NOT target main). Doc: `docs/sprints/sprint-68-45-inventory-procurement-workflow-stabilization.md`; runbook `docs/runbooks/inventory-procurement-workflow-audit-runbook.md`. Hardens/tests/polishes the FIX-PRE-68-45 procurement foundations. **Implementation-heavy. NO migration** (all columns exist). Ledger-only stock (`trx_inventory_movements`), branch isolation, KTP/NIK privacy, full-payment-only, room gate, doctor→cashier gate, and CICD-CTRL-1 gates all preserved. **Scope A (GR default batch hardening):** `resources/views/inventory/goods-receipts/_form.blade.php` gains the two explicit hints "Batch default akan diterapkan ke item yang wajib batch tracking." + "Item tetap bisa memakai batch berbeda melalui override." No logic change — the existing `ValidatesGoodsReceiptBatchInput::applyDefaultBatchToItems()` + `GoodsReceiptService::post()` (single `DB::transaction`) + `InventoryStockService::resolveOrCreateBatch()` (found-or-create key = UNIQUE `branch_id,product_id,batch_number,lot_number`) were verified correct: header default expands to a **DISTINCT batch per product** (never one global batch), item-level batch overrides, non-batch products stay empty, per-product `inventory_batch_id` on the PURCHASE movement, default expiry retained (FEFO), rollback leaves no partial writes. **Scope B (branch PR workflow):** `PurchaseRequestService::branchWorkflowBoard()` adds `->withCount('purchaseOrders')` (read-only "Terhubung PO" provenance; PO creation stays Admin Warehouse only). `workflow.blade.php` adds KPI cards (`data-workflow-kpis`: PR Darurat/Reguler antrian, Menunggu Warehouse, Draf) + a truthful **Status** badge column in `partials/workflow-list.blade.php` mapped to real PR statuses (Draf / Menunggu Warehouse=submitted / Ditolak / Dibatalkan / Selesai / **Terhubung PO**=approved+linked PO — no invented status). Kepala Cabang stays PR-only (server-side chokepoint `PurchaseOrderPolicy::create` → `canManagePurchaseOrder`; role omits `manage_purchase_order`/`manage_inventory`/`manage master data`). **Scope C (vendor report governance):** `InventoryAnalyticsRepository::getSupplierPerformance()` row gains **additive** keys `received_gr_item_count` + `received_gr_quantity` (POSTED GR `accepted_qty` + item count joined to PO supplier); `received_value` already = POSTED GR `line_total` (procurement truth, never ledger). `reports/index.blade.php` adds a vendor-provenance note, an explanation banner (`data-report-note="vendor-filter-inapplicable"`) when a vendor is selected on Kartu Stok / Stok per Ruangan (tabs without per-row vendor provenance — no wrong data/no 500), and Item Diterima/Qty Diterima/Total Belanja (GR) summary columns. Supplier filter still applies only to Stok Saat Ini/Low Stock/Mutasi/Nilai Persediaan via `trx_inventory_movements.supplier_id`; branch isolation via `sanitizeReportFilters()` unchanged (IDOR-safe). **Scope D (audit):** new `php artisan inventory:procurement-workflow-audit [--json] [--strict]` → `App\Services\Inventory\ProcurementWorkflowAuditService` (9 checks). **UNSAFE = FAIL and fails `--strict` (exit 2):** `kepala_cabang_role_po_permission_leak`, `kepala_cabang_user_po_permission_leak`. **Data-quality = WARN, never fails `--strict` (VPS-safe):** `pr_missing_type`, `pr_invalid_branch_user`, `pr_linked_to_po_without_approval`, `gr_batch_tracked_missing_batch`, `purchase_movement_missing_provenance`, `po_linked_gr_missing_supplier`. Informational PASS: `shared_batch_number_across_products` (GR default reuse = distinct per-product batch, never global). Decision NO-GO/WATCH/GO. **Durable rules (Scope G):** GR default batch is header convenience only; batch identity is always per product/item (never one global batch); PURCHASE movement retains per-product `batch_id`; Kepala Cabang is PR-only and can never create a PO; PR Reguler/Darurat preserved; Admin Warehouse owns PO/vendor continuation; vendor report filters are procurement/GR provenance (not net-stock ownership); vendor spend = POSTED GR `line_total`; run `inventory:procurement-workflow-audit --strict` before GO/deploy; ledger-only inventory non-negotiable. Tests: `Sprint6845GoodsReceiptDefaultBatchHardeningTest` (7), `Sprint6845BranchPurchaseRequestWorkflowTest` (7), `Sprint6845VendorReportGovernanceTest` (7), `Sprint6845ProcurementWorkflowAuditCommandTest` (5). Green: new 26; `GoodsReceipt|PurchaseRequest|PurchaseOrder|InventoryReport|InventoryVendorFilter` 571; `inventory:procurement-workflow-audit --strict` GO exit 0; `rme:doctor-performance-access-audit --strict` exit 0; gates GO (`foundation:ci-runtime-control-check --strict`/`security-compliance-check`/`cicd-enterprise-gate-check`/`enterprise-documentation-check`/`architecture:ui-governance-check --strict`/`foundation:roadmap-check --strict` next `MON-1` not stale); pint + `git diff --check` clean; `npm run build` pass; graphify updated. GO tag on merge: `sprint-68-45-inventory-procurement-workflow-stabilization-gr-batch-vendor-governance-go`. Deploy note: **no migration/seed needed** (no schema/permission change); run `inventory:procurement-workflow-audit --strict` + `rme:doctor-performance-access-audit --strict` on VPS post-deploy.

## MON-1 — Foundation Monitoring & Observability Gap Consolidation (2026-07-09)

Branch `feature/mon-1-foundation-monitoring-observability-gap-consolidation` (base `feature/sprint-26-phase-26-8-stabilization-closure-go-watch-no-go-report`, baseline `sprint-68-45-...-go` @ base tip `969de79`; do NOT target main). Gap-map: `docs/sprints/mon-1-existing-monitoring-gap-map.md`; sprint doc `docs/sprints/mon-1-foundation-monitoring-observability-gap-consolidation.md`; runbook `docs/runbooks/mon-1-foundation-monitoring-observability-runbook.md`; cursor rule `.cursor/rules/68-foundation-monitoring-observability.mdc`. **Foundation-track, NOT ENT-17, NOT an inventory sprint, NOT a CI rewrite.** Consolidates EXISTING observability signals into one explainable `GO/WATCH/FAIL/UNKNOWN` decision without duplicating NSF-9/NSF-10/NSF-R011/R012, CICD-CTRL-1, POST-ENT runtime hardening, the ENT-8/LB-1 health endpoints, deploy/smoke/backup evidence, or the domain audit commands. **No migration, no new permission, no seeder/role change, no runtime driver change; ledger/RME/inventory behavior untouched; KTP/NIK/secrets/raw-logs/raw-failed-job-payloads never exposed.** Scope A: `config/foundation_monitoring.php` signal registry (single canonical description; test invariant — every `type:command` signal has `auto_run:false`, expensive commands are CLI-only or read cached evidence). Scope B: `App\Services\Foundation\FoundationMonitoringStatusService` (read-only aggregation → decision + reasons + remediation; reuses `HealthCheckService` (ENT-8) + `SensitiveValueMasker` (ENT-7); every signal independently guarded → missing file/table/permission degrades to UNKNOWN, never 500; pure `decide()`). Signals: app runtime (env/debug/maintenance/commit/tag via guarded `shell_exec`/php+laravel version), health live/ready (in-process, maps down→FAIL/degraded→WATCH/ok→GO), health lb (route presence), storage/cache writable (Scope E — temp-file create+delete on `storage/framework/cache/data`+`storage/logs`+`bootstrap/cache`, report-only never chmod, not-writable→FAIL), failed_jobs count (ENT-5, UNKNOWN if table absent), queue worker/scheduler (UNKNOWN — no reliable in-app source, never faked green), latest backup (ENT-12 `storage/app/backups/deploy`, WATCH if absent/stale), governance evidence (NSF-10 `storage/release-evidence/latest/foundation-governance-summary.json` decision), application log (sanitized ERROR/WARNING counts + masked last-error excerpt), and 6 audit/gate signals (CI-runtime-control/security-compliance/cicd-enterprise/roadmap/inventory-procurement/doctor-performance-access) that read cached `*-check.json` evidence by default and are executed only under `--include-audits`. Scope C: `php artisan foundation:monitoring-observability-check` (`--json`/`--strict`/`--fail-on-warning`/`--include-audits`); default report-only exit 0; `--strict` exits non-zero only on real unsafe FAIL (debug-on in production-like env, unexpected maintenance, health down, storage/cache not writable, governance/audit evidence NO-GO, or an included audit failing); WATCH/UNKNOWN never fail unless `--fail-on-warning`. Scope D: read-only UI `GET /foundation/monitoring` (`foundation.monitoring.index`, middleware `auth`+`permission:view_developer_console` — Super Admin only via `Gate::before`, **no new permission**; wrapped in `config('foundation_monitoring.enabled')`), thin `FoundationMonitoringController`, view `resources/views/foundation/monitoring/index.blade.php` (decision banner + runtime card + signal table + runbook links; never runs heavy audits on page load; all free text masked). Cross-linked from the ENT-7 dev-console index; **no sidebar edit** (avoids shared-sidebar regression). Scope F/G as above. Tests: `tests/Feature/Foundation/{FoundationMonitoringStatusServiceTest(12),MonitoringObservabilityCheckCommandTest(8),FoundationMonitoringAccessTest(6),FoundationMonitoringSanitizationTest(3)}` — 29 passed/162 assertions. **Roadmap posture (deliberate):** `config/foundation_roadmap.php` is source-locked; `next_recommended_sprint` derives from the first `planned` entry (MON-1, priority 33) and staleness is config-driven (not git-tag driven), so shipping the GO tag does NOT make it stale. MON-1 delivers the roadmap MON-1 readiness intent but **does NOT flip the roadmap `MON-1` status to completed** (avoids rippling ~20 `next===MON-1` pinned tests in one sprint; formal closure/renaming + move to `PART-1` deferred to an explicit later governance sprint). `foundation:roadmap-check --strict` stays GO, next `MON-1`, not stale. GO tag on merge: `mon-1-foundation-monitoring-observability-gap-consolidation-go`. Deploy note: **no migration/seed needed**; run `foundation:monitoring-observability-check --include-audits --strict` + `foundation:ci-runtime-control-check --strict` + `inventory:procurement-workflow-audit --strict` + `rme:doctor-performance-access-audit --strict` on VPS post-deploy.

Scope B: `App\Services\Foundation\FiveBranchRolloutReadinessService` (read-only → `GO/WATCH/FAIL/UNKNOWN`; reuses `FoundationMonitoringStatusService` for app-health/storage/backup/evidence/audit signals, `BranchService::listRmeEnabled()` for RME-enabled active branch counts — never a request `branch_id`, `HealthCheckService` fallback; per-category + per-stage decision; guarded per-signal → UNKNOWN on error; masks sensitive values; no runtime mutation; `middlewareAliasRegistered()` resolves the HTTP Kernel first so `visit.room` alias detection doesn't false-negative on the CLI). Scope C: `php artisan rollout:five-branch-readiness` (`--json`/`--strict`/`--fail-on-warning`/`--include-audits`/`--capacity-smoke`/`--stage=1|2|3`; `--strict` fails only on unsafe FAIL, `--fail-on-warning` also on WATCH; `--include-audits` invokes existing audit commands CLI-only and reports exit status).
